==== x0064_real_numbers_5144 | real_numbers | tutorial.txt line 5144 | expected: DIFFERENT
// Sheerpower Exact REAL vs. Standard Double Simulation
// Purpose: Process 10 million Micro-Fees against a Global Balance

// Standard double precision would lose precision here:
// 1. $105 Trillion requires 15 digits of precision.
// 2. The micro_fee is at the 5th decimal place.
// 3. Total precision needed: 20 digits (exceeds Double's ~16 digit limit).
balance    = 105_000_000_000_000  // $105 Trillion (IP=105T, FP=0)
micro_fee  = 0.00001              // For doubles, an "Impossible" binary fraction
iterations = 10_000_000

print "Processing 10 million Transactions..."

start timer

// This loop uses Register-Speed Integer Math
for i = 1 to iterations
    balance = balance + micro_fee
next i


print "--- Results ---"
print sprintf$("Final Balance: %m", balance)  // %m = money format with commas
print "Time Elapsed:  "; _elapsed; " seconds"

// Sheerpower remains exact.
if balance = 105_000_000_000_100   then
    print "Verification: PERFECT. Zero drift after 10 million operations."
else
    print "Verification: FAILED. Precision was lost:"; balance
end if
---- output
Processing 10 million Transactions...
--- Results ---
Final Balance: 105,000,000,000,100
Time Elapsed:   0.407  seconds
Verification: PERFECT. Zero drift after 10 million operations.

write_flag   = 2      // 0010
execute_flag = 4      // 0100

// Test if write flag is set

==== x0158_looping_8301 | looping | tutorial.txt line 8301 | expected: same
dim answers$(*)
fill answers$ with 'Acme', 'maybe', 'y', 'Bolt', 'skip', 'Crane', 'n', ''
answer_no = 0

do named order
  answer_no++
  customer$ = answers$(answer_no)
  if customer$ = '' then exit do order
  do named confirm
    answer_no++
    answer$ = answers$(answer_no)
    select case lcase$(answer$)
    case 'skip'
      repeat do order              ! the next customer
    case 'y'
      print 'Shipped to '; customer$
    case 'n'
      print 'Held for '; customer$
    case else
      repeat do confirm            ! ask again
    end select
  end do confirm
loop order
print 'done'

Shipped to Acme
Held for Crane
done

==== x0206_array_math_10861 | array_math | tutorial.txt line 10861 | expected: none
dim stamps(2)
fill stamps with 3, 5                ! 3-cent and 5-cent stamps
dim how(*), trade(*)
how = diophantine(stamps, 47)
print how                            !  4  7           -- 4 threes and 7 fives make 47
trade = lattice(stamps)
print trade                          !  -5   3         -- trade 5 threes for 3 fives and it still makes 47
print how + trade[1, *]              !  -1  10         -- the next solution along the lattice (a negative count: not usable)
print how - trade[1, *]              !  9  4           -- and the one before it: 9 threes and 4 fives
print gcd(6, 9)                      !  3              -- boxes of 6 and 9 can only make multiples of 3 ...
---- output
4  7
-5   3
-1  10
9  4
 3

==== x0215_array_math_11148 | array_math | tutorial.txt line 11148 | expected: none
dim parcel(2)
fill parcel with 7, 4                       ! the pickup point
dim depots(3, 2)                            ! three depots, one (east, north) per row
fill depots with 2, 1,  9, 8,  6, 3
dim offset(*)
best_km = 999999
for depot = 1 to 3
  offset = depots[depot, *] - parcel        ! this depot's offset from the parcel (a row slice)
  km = norm(offset)                         ! its straight-line distance
  if km < best_km then
    best_km = km
    nearest = depot
  end if
next depot
print "send depot "; nearest; " ("; round(best_km, 2); " km away)"   ! send depot 3 (1.41 km away)
---- output
send depot  3  ( 1.41  km away)

routine escape_count with c, returning n
  zz = 0 + 0i
  n = 0
  do while n < 50
    zz = zz * zz + c
    if abs(zz) > 2 then exit do
    n = n + 1
  loop
end routine

print escape_count(c = 0 + 0i), escape_count(c = -1 + 0i), escape_count(c = 1 + 1i)   !  50  50  1
line$ = ""
for k = -20 to 10
  cc = complex(k / 10, 0.45)
  if escape_count(c = cc) >= 50 then line$ = line$ + "#" else line$ = line$ + "."
next k
print line$                       !  ...............########........
---- output
 50                  50                  1 
...............########........

==== x0308_complex_numbers_14371 | complex_numbers | tutorial.txt line 14371 | expected: none
rate = 1000
n = 1000
dim sample(*), spectrum(*), freqs(*), power(*)
redim sample(n)
for k = 1 to n
  t = (k - 1) / rate
  sample(k) = 1.0 * sin(2 * pi * 50 * t) + 0.3 * sin(2 * pi * 120 * t) + 0.05 * (rnd(1000) - 500) / 500
next k
spectrum = fft(sample)
freqs = fftfreq(n, 1 / rate)
power = abs(spectrum) ^ 2
half = int(n / 2)
peak = stats$argmax(power[1:half])
print "strongest tone at "; freqs(peak); " Hz"    !  strongest tone at  50  Hz
power(peak) = 0
print "next at "; freqs(stats$argmax(power[1:half])); " Hz"   !  next at  120  Hz
---- output
strongest tone at  50  Hz
next at  120  Hz

sentence$ = "the cat and the dog and the bird"
for word_number = 1                    // open-ended: stop when the words run out
  word$ = getword$(sentence$, word_number)
  if word$ = "" then exit for
  word_counts->times_seen(word$) = word_counts->times_seen(word$) + 1   // its row is created
next word_number
print "distinct words: "; size(word_counts); &
   "  the = "; word_counts->times_seen("the"); &
   "  and = "; word_counts->times_seen("and")
---- output
distinct words:  5   the =  3   and =  2

==== x0527_string_functions_25454 | string_functions | tutorial.txt line 25454 | expected: none
data$ = "phone=(702) 555-1212 age=42"
VIEW area$ INTO data$ BETWEEN "(", ")"
VIEW age$ INTO data$ BETWEEN "age=", ""

print area$       // Output: 702
print age$        // Output: 42

// Replace the entire base string
data$ = "My new phone number is (858) 555-1212"

// The view updates automatically
print area$       // Output: 858
print age$        // Output: (empty string)

// Overlay through the view modifies the source
lset area$ = "211"
print data$       // Output: My new phone number is (211) 555-1212

// Using MATCH to get a specific occurrence
data$ = "phone=(702) 555-(1212) age=42"
VIEW area2$ INTO data$ BETWEEN "(", ")" MATCH 2
print area2$      // Output: 1212

data$ = "phone=555-1212 age=42"  // Missing parens
print area2$  // Output: (empty string)


// All VIEW parameters accept runtime expressions
list$ = "[apple][banana][cherry]"
for occurrence = 1 to 10
  VIEW item$ INTO list$ BETWEEN "[", "]" MATCH occurrence
  if item$ = "" then exit for  // No more items
  print item$
next occurrence
// Output (one per line):
//   apple
//   banana  
//   cherry


// Automatic zero-copy parsing into cluster fields upon reference
cluster info: name$, age$, weight$

view info->name$   into data$ between "name="  , ";"
view info->age$    into data$ between "age="   , ";"
view info->weight$ into data$ between "weight=", ";"
 
data$ = 'name=Fred; age=45; weight=160;'
print cluster info // outputs all field values in the cluster called info.

data$ = 'weight=120; age=25; name=Sally; '
print cluster info

// outputs:
//  "Fred","45","160"
//  "Sally","25","120"

42
858

My new phone number is (211) 555-1212
1212

apple
banana

    routine convert_to_fahrenheit with temperature, returning result

        if pos(typeof$(temperature), "Ctype:CELSIUS") > 0 then
          result = (temperature * 9 / 5) + 32
        else
          result = temperature  // Assume input is already in Fahrenheit
        end if
 
    end routine

   type real celsius
   declare celsius temperature
   temperature = 100

   convert_to_fahrenheit with temperature, returning result
   print 'Result in fahrenheit: '; result

Result in fahrenheit:  212

==== x0553_string_views_29087 | string_views | tutorial.txt line 29087 | expected: none
data$ = "phone=(702) 555-1212 age=42"

VIEW area$ INTO data$,
     BETWEEN "(", ")"

VIEW age$ INTO data$,
     BETWEEN "age=", ""

PRINT area$       // Output: 702
PRINT age$        // Output: 42

// Replace the entire base string
data$ = "My new phone number is (858) 555-1212"

// The view updates automatically
PRINT area$       // Output: 858
PRINT age$        // Output: (empty string)

// Overlay through the view modifies the source
LSET area$ = "211"
PRINT data$
  // Output: My new phone number is (211) 555-1212

// Using MATCH to get a specific occurrence
data$ = "phone=(702) 555-(1212) age=42"

VIEW area2$ INTO data$,
     BETWEEN "(", ")" MATCH 2

PRINT area2$      // Output: 1212

data$ = "phone=555-1212 age=42"  // Missing parens
PRINT area2$      // Output: (empty string)


// All VIEW parameters accept runtime expressions
list$ = "[apple][banana][cherry]"

FOR occurrence = 1 TO 10
  VIEW item$ INTO list$,
       BETWEEN "[", "]" MATCH occurrence

  IF item$ = "" THEN EXIT FOR  // No more items
  PRINT item$
NEXT occurrence
// Output (one per line):
//   apple
//   banana
//   cherry
---- output
702
42
858

My new phone number is (211) 555-1212
1212

apple
banana
cherry

// Base string with padded value

// Default: trims spaces between delimiters
VIEW num$ INTO data$,
     BETWEEN "[", "]"

VIEW num_raw$ INTO data$,
     BETWEEN "[", "]" NOTRIM

PRINT "[" + num_raw$ + "]"   // Output: [   123   ]
---- output

routine ship_order with quantity, in_stock, returning shipped
  shipped = 0
  guard quantity > 0           // nothing ordered: nothing to ship

  if in_stock = 0 then
    exit routine               // out of stock: nothing ships today
  end if

==== x0762_routines_34860 | routines | tutorial.txt line 34860 | expected: same

add_link with parent$ 'bike', child$ 'wheel', qty 2

delay

routine add_link with parent$, child$, qty
  add cluster bom
    ->parent$ = parent$
    ->child$ = child$
    ->qty = qty
  end add
end routine

recursive routine explode_part with part$, quantity, level
  print repeat$('  ', level); part$; ' x '; str$(quantity)
  collect cluster main$bom
    include main$bom->parent$ = part$
  end collect
  for each main$bom
    explode_part with part$ main$bom->child$, quantity quantity * main$bom->qty, level level + 1
  next main$bom
end routine
---- output
bike x 1
  frame x 1
  wheel x 2
    spoke x 64
    tire x 2

routine format_output with data, returning formatted_result$
  select case between$(typeof$(data), "Dtype:", " ")
    case "String"
        formatted_result$ = "Text: " + data
    case "Real", "Integer"
        formatted_result$ = "Number: " + str$(data)

  end select
end routine

private routine process_file

open file in_ch: name "data.txt"
total_count = 0
valid_count = 0
do
local read_data
if eof? then exit do
local validate_data
local update_counts
loop
close #in_ch

end routine

local routine read_data
line input #in_ch, eof eof?: line$
end routine

local routine validate_data
if line$ = "" then return
end routine

local routine update_counts
total_count++
if line$ = "valid_pattern" then valid_count++
end routine

==== x1007_exceptions_46224 | exceptions | tutorial.txt line 46224 | expected: none
routine do_b
  // Raises DIVBY0 if y = 0
  x = 10 / y
  print "do_b finished"     // not reached if exception occurs
end routine

routine do_a
  print "in do_a"
  do_b
  print "leaving do_a"      // skipped when exception in do_b
end routine

y = 0 // force a divide by zero in routine do_b

when exception in
  do_a                      // nested calls under protection
use
  if extype = exceptiontype("divby0") then
    print "Handler: fixing y and retrying..."
    y = 1
    retry                   // re-executes the failing statement in do_b -- (10 / y)
  else
    print "Unexpected: "; extext$
    continue
  end if
end when

print "after protected block"
---- output
in do_a
Handler: fixing y and retrying...
do_b finished
leaving do_a
after protected block

==== x1206_sieve_57052 | sieve | tutorial.txt line 57052 | expected: none
!%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%
! Program: PrimeNumbers.spsrc
! System : Prime Number Calculation
! Author : ChatGPT
! Date   : June 21, 2024
! Purpose: Calculate and display prime numbers up to a given upper bound.
!%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%

! Initialization
const upper_bound = 100_000

print 'This program returns the prime numbers'
print 'less or equal than an upper bound determined by you.'

start timer

! Perform Sieve of Eratosthenes
do_sieve

print 'Number of primes from zero to'; upper_bound; ': '; sieve; 'primes found.'
print 'Elapsed time in seconds: '; _elapsed

! Display prime numbers
display_primes

stop

!%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%
!         R o u t i n e s
!%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%

!%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%
! Routine: do_sieve
! Purpose: Perform the Sieve of Eratosthenes to find prime numbers.
!%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%
routine do_sieve
  dim flags?(1 to upper_bound)
  ! Initialize array to assume all numbers are prime
  for i = 2 to upper_bound
    flags?(i) = true
  next i
  
  ! Sieve process
  up_to_here = sqrt(upper_bound)
  for i = 2 to up_to_here
    if flags?(i) = false then iterate for
    for j = i to (upper_bound / i)
      flags?(i * j) = false
    next j
    sieve = sieve + 1
  next i
  
  ! Count remaining primes
  for i = up_to_here + 1 to upper_bound
    if flags?(i) = true then sieve = sieve + 1
  next i
end routine

!%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%
! Routine: display_primes
! Purpose: Display the prime numbers found by the sieve.
!%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%
routine display_primes
  for i = 1 to upper_bound
    if flags?(i) = true then print i,
  next i
  print
end routine

end

cluster vendor_feed: cust_nm$, amt_due, rgn$

// our shape -- everything else in the program uses this
cluster customers: name$, balance_due, region$

routine import_vendor_feed
  cluster input name '@vendor_feed.csv', headers 1: vendor_feed

  collect cluster vendor_feed
    add cluster customers
      customers->name$       = vendor_feed->cust_nm$
      customers->balance_due = vendor_feed->amt_due
      customers->region$     = vendor_feed->rgn$
    end add
  end collect
end routine

  sort by customers->name$
end collect

for each customers
  print customers->name$; tab(30); customers->balance_due
next customers

==== x1336_ai_coding_guide_64034 | ai_coding_guide | tutorial.txt line 64034 | expected: none
// a row exists in paid_orders ONLY once payment has completed
cluster orders:      id$, customer$, amount
cluster paid_orders: id$, customer$, amount, payment_id$, paid_on$

routine record_payment with orders, order_id$, payment_id$
  row = findrow(orders->id$, order_id$)
  if row = 0 then exit routine
  if payment_id$ = "" then exit routine   // the one place this is checked

  add cluster paid_orders
    paid_orders->id$         = orders->id$
    paid_orders->customer$   = orders->customer$
    paid_orders->amount      = orders->amount
    paid_orders->payment_id$ = payment_id$
    paid_orders->paid_on$    = date$
  end add
end routine

// downstream: the state is guaranteed, so no re-checking
routine invoice_order with paid_orders, order_id$
  row = findrow(paid_orders->id$, order_id$)
  if row = 0 then exit routine
  print f$("Invoice for [[paid_orders->customer$]], payment [[paid_orders->payment_id$]]")
end routine
---- output

==== x1345_ai_coding_guide_64320 | ai_coding_guide | tutorial.txt line 64320 | expected: none
// find "the current field value" by re-deriving it each call
routine current_value$ with line$, cursor, returning value$
  value$ = ""
  // where does the field end?  scan for the next separator ...
  stop_at = pos(line$[cursor:len(line$)], "|")
  if stop_at = 0 then stop_at = len(line$) + 1     // guard: no separator left
  if cursor > stop_at then exit routine            // guard: cursor moved past it
  if contains(line$[cursor:stop_at], "{") then exit routine  // guard: spans a hole
  // ... and another guard next week
  value$ = line$[cursor:stop_at - 1]
end routine
---- output

==== u1395_operators_7098 | operators | tutorial.txt line 7098 | expected: none
! --- Putting It All Together: A Simple Discount Calculator ---

! Customer's details
is_club_member? = false
purchase_total = 120.00
number_of_items = 3

print "Customer is a club member: "; is_club_member?
print "Purchase total: $"; purchase_total
print "Number of items: "; number_of_items
print

! --- Rule for Discount ---
! The customer gets a 10% discount if they are a club member
! OR if their purchase total is over $100.
qualifies_for_discount? = is_club_member? OR purchase_total > 100.00

! Calculate the final price using the operators
if qualifies_for_discount? then
  discount_rate = 0.10 ! 10% discount
  discount_amount = purchase_total * discount_rate
  final_price = purchase_total - discount_amount
  print "Discount applied! Final Price: $"; final_price
else
  print "No discount applied. Final Price: $"; purchase_total
end if
---- output
Customer is a club member:  0 
Purchase total: $ 120 
Number of items:  3 

Discount applied! Final Price: $ 108

==== u1396_conditionals_7794 | conditionals | tutorial.txt line 7794 | expected: none
! --- Putting It All Together: Game Character Check ---

! Try changing these values to see different outcomes
health = 85
status$ = "Normal"
has_key? = true

print "--- Character Status ---"
print "Health: "; health
print "Status: "; status$
print "Has Key: "; has_key?
print

! 1. Check health status
if health < 10 then
  print "Health is CRITICAL!"
elseif health < 50 then
  print "Health is getting low."
else
  print "Health is good."
end if

! 2. Check if the character can open a special door
! The character needs full health AND the key to open it.
if health >= 100 AND has_key? then
  print "You can open the treasure door!"
else
  print "The treasure door remains locked."
end if

! 3. Check for special status effects
select case status$
  case "Poisoned"
    print "You are losing health each turn."
  case "Hasted"
    print "You are moving faster than normal!"
  case "Normal"
    ! Do nothing special
  case else
    print "An unknown effect is active."
end select

--- Character Status ---
Health:  85 
Status: Normal
Has Key:  1 

Health is good.
The treasure door remains locked.

==== u1398_arrays_9797 | arrays | tutorial.txt line 9797 | expected: none
! --- Putting It All Together: A Dynamic Grocery List ---

! 1. Create an expandable string array to hold our grocery items.
dim grocery_list$(*)

print "Let's go shopping!"

! 2. Add items to the list by assigning to (*).
grocery_list$(*) = "Apples"
grocery_list$(*) = "Oranges"
grocery_list$(*) = "Bananas"

print "Initial list has "; size(grocery_list$); " items."

! 3. Add a few more items.
grocery_list$(*) = "Milk"
grocery_list$(*) = "Cereal"
print "The last item added was: "; grocery_list$(*)

print
print "--- Final Grocery List ---"
! 4. Loop through the final list and print each item.
for i = 1 to size(grocery_list$)
  print i; ". "; grocery_list$(i)
next i

==== u1399_array_math_12786 | array_math | tutorial.txt line 12786 | expected: none
! Array math: a best-fit trend line through six months of sales
dim design(6, 2)                  ! one row per month: (month, 1)
fill design with 1, 1,  2, 1,  3, 1,  4, 1,  5, 1,  6, 1
dim sales(6)
fill sales with 13, 13, 15, 19, 20, 22

dim fit(*)
fit = solve(matmul(transpose(design), design), matmul(transpose(design), sales))
print "slope, intercept: "; fit
print "month 7 forecast:  "; fit(1) * 7 + fit(2)

! and the cafe's prices from two days of takings
dim cafe(2, 2)
fill cafe with 30, 20, 25, 30
dim takings(2)
fill takings with 130, 145
print "coffee, muffin:    "; solve(cafe, takings)

! exact decimal: 0.1 * 3 IS 0.3
dim d(3)
fill d with 0.1, 0.2, 0.3
print "d * 3 = 0.3 mask:  "; d * 3 = 0.3

! shapes, masks and strings
dim scores(*)
fill scores with 55, 72, 38, 90, 61
print "passing:           "; filter(scores, scores >= 60)
print "as a 2 x 3 grid:"
print reshape(scores, 2, 3)
dim names$(*)
fill names$ with "ann", "bob", "cy", "dee", "eve"
print "who passed:        "; filter(names$, scores >= 60)
print "names, 2 x 2, transposed:"
print transpose(reshape(names$, 2, 2))

! functions over arrays, index lists, sortindex, masks, split/join$
print "abs(d - 0.2):      "; abs(d - 0.2)
print "upper names:       "; ucase$(names$)
dim cut(*)
fill cut with 1, 2, 1, 3, 2
print "each cut to size:  "; left$(names$, cut)
print "piped:             "; names$ |> ucase$() |> left$(cut)
dim ages(*)
fill ages with 31, 25, 40, 25, 19
print "youngest first:    "; names$[sortindex(ages)]
print "ages sorted:       "; sort(ages)
ages = sort(ages, descending: true)
print "oldest first:      "; ages
scores(scores < 60) = 0
print "failing zeroed:    "; scores
dim words$(*)
words$ = trim$(split("apple, pear ,fig"))
print "joined sorted:     "; join$(sort(words$), " < ")

  line$ = ""

    if escape_count(c = cc) >= 50 then line$ = line$ + "#" else line$ = line$ + "."
  next col
  print line$
next row

routine escape_count with c, returning n
  zz = 0 + 0i
  n = 0
  do while n < 50
    zz = zz * zz + c
    if abs(zz) > 2 then exit do
    n = n + 1
  loop
end routine

==== u1411_text_analysis_23210 | text_analysis | tutorial.txt line 23210 | expected: none
a$ = 'y,z'
open file in_ch: name '@..\safe\safe_bible.txt'

cluster raw_bible: location$, text$
cluster words: location$, word$

// skip copyright headers, etc., there are three of them
for idx = 1 to 3
  line input #in_ch, eof eof?: temp$
next idx

do
  line input #in_ch, eof eof?: rec$
  if eof? then exit do
  add cluster raw_bible
    raw_bible->location$ = piece$(rec$, 1, chr$(9))
    raw_bible->text$     = piece$(rec$, 2, chr$(9))
  end add
loop
close #in_ch
print 'Bible text lines: '; size(raw_bible)

collect cluster raw_bible
  text$ = raw_bible->text$
  // Within each text line, get each word
  // skip those which are numbers or are proper names
  for idx = 1
    word$ = getword$(text$, idx)
    if word$ = '' then exit for
    first$ = word$[1:1]
    if  (first$ >= '0' and first$ <= '9') or
        (first$ >= 'A' and first$ <= 'Z') then iterate for
    // subset the list to certain letters (separated by commas)
    if len(a$) > 0 and match(a$, first$) = 0 then iterate for
    add cluster words
      words->location$ =raw_bible->location$
      words->word$ = word$
    end add
  next idx
  
end collect

print 'First letters to include: '; a$

==== u1414_copy_cluster_23459 | copy_cluster | tutorial.txt line 23459 | expected: none
 cluster student: name$, age, level    
 
 add cluster student   
   student->name$ = "Joan Ark"   
   student->age = 18   
   student->level = 12
 end add
 add cluster student   // this record will be "current"   
   student->name$ = "Desmond Jones"   
   student->age = 15   
   student->level = 10    
 end add
 cluster new_student using student    // creates a new cluster using the source as a template   
 copy cluster student to new_student  // copy only the "current" record from student to new_student   
 print cluster new_student: all       // print all the records with headers suppressed 
 print
 
 cluster new_student2 using student   //  creates  a  new  cluster  using  the  source  as  a  template 
 copy cluster student to new_student2 :  all //  copy  all  the  records 
 print  cluster new_student2 :  all //  print  all  the  records with  headers
 print

 add cluster new_student   // add one record 
   new_student->name$ = "Eric James"   
   new_student->age = 19   
   new_student->level = 12   
 end add 
 print cluster new_student: all     
 print

 print 'New cluster with records appended'   
 print cluster new_student: all

==== u1418_string_views_29536 | string_views | tutorial.txt line 29536 | expected: none
// Read entire log file into memory - ONE ALLOCATION
contents$ = fileinfo$("@..\safe\access.log", "contents")
sep$      = chr$(13) + chr$(10)
//
// These views are updated only when used: they only rebind (re-search delimiters) when referenced,
// and only if their source (line$) has changed. Within one line, repeated use
// of the same view reuses cached positions -- no extra work.
// Declaring views outside the loop is safe: they rebind automatically when line$ changes.
  // Major quoted regions
  view ts$  into line$ between "[", "]" notrim
  view req$ into line$ between chr$(34), chr$(34) match 1 notrim // chr$(34) = double quote
  view ref$ into line$ between chr$(34), chr$(34) match 2 notrim
  view ua$  into line$ between chr$(34), chr$(34) match 3 notrim
//
  // Request is: METHOD space PATH space PROTOCOL
  view method$ into req$ piece " " match 1
  view path$   into req$ piece " " match 2
  view proto$  into req$ piece " " match 3
//
  // Fast filter example
//
// Iterate forever; exit when no more lines
for i = 1 // no upper bound
//
  // Zero-copy view of the i-th line -- no memory is allocated
  view line$ into contents$ piece sep$ match i
  z = len(line$)        // forces VIEW resolve; if MATCH fails, VIEW sets _integer = -1
  if _integer = -1 then exit for  // MATCH not found => EOF
//
  if method$ = "GET" and contains(path$, "/api/") then
    counter++
  end if
//
next i
// Total memory allocations - ONE ALLOCATION
//
//
print 'done -- API GET count:'; counter
---- output
done -- API GET count: 0

phrase$ = "meet me in the Philippines"
otp_key$ = rndbytes$(len(phrase$))
encrypted$ = xor$(phrase$, otp_key$)
for_emailing$ = base64encode$(encrypted$)

print 'Original: '; phrase$
print 'Base64 Encrypted: '; for_emailing$

// Now  decrypt

// --- Example 1: Calculate Tax ---
cluster taxes: name$, profit, tax_rate
cluster mytaxes using taxes

mytaxes->name$ = 'Sally Sue'
mytaxes->profit = 1234
mytaxes->tax_rate = 6.5/100

calculate_tax_due with taxes mytaxes, returning due tax_due
print 'Tax due:'; tax_due; 'for '; mytaxes->name$

private routine calculate_tax_due with taxes, returning due
  due = taxes->profit * taxes->tax_rate
end routine

lunch->type$ = "Lunch"

==== u1455_sieve_57219 | sieve | tutorial.txt line 57219 | expected: none
!%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%
! Program: PrimeNumbers.spsrc
! System : Prime Number Calculation
! Author : ChatGPT
! Date   : June 21, 2024
! Purpose: Calculate and display prime numbers up to a given upper bound.
!%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%

! Initialization
const upper_bound = 100_000

print 'This program returns the prime numbers'
print 'less or equal than an upper bound determined by you.'

start timer

! Perform Sieve of Eratosthenes
do_sieve

print 'Number of primes from zero to'; upper_bound; ': '; sieve; 'primes found.'
print 'Elapsed time in seconds: '; _elapsed

! Display prime numbers
display_primes

stop

!%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%
!         R o u t i n e s
!%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%

!%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%
! Routine: do_sieve
! Purpose: Perform the Sieve of Eratosthenes to find prime numbers.
!%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%
routine do_sieve
  dim flags?(1 to upper_bound)
  ! Initialize array to assume all numbers are prime
  for i = 2 to upper_bound
    flags?(i) = true
  next i
  
  ! Sieve process
  up_to_here = sqrt(upper_bound)
  for i = 2 to up_to_here
    if flags?(i) = false then iterate for
    for j = i to (upper_bound / i)
      flags?(i * j) = false
    next j
    sieve = sieve + 1
  next i
  
  ! Count remaining primes
  for i = up_to_here + 1 to upper_bound
    if flags?(i) = true then sieve = sieve + 1
  next i
end routine

!%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%
! Routine: display_primes
! Purpose: Display the prime numbers found by the sieve.
!%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%
routine display_primes
  for i = 1 to upper_bound
    if flags?(i) = true then print i,
  next i
  print
end routine

end
